============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

   195	function PhonePreview({
   196	  title,
   197	  metric,
   198	  variant,
   199	  href,
   200	  screenImageSrc,
   201	  screenImageAlt,
   202	}: {
   203	  href?: string;
   204	  title: string;
   205	  metric: string;
   206	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   207	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   208	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   209	}) {
   210	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   211	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   212	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   213	  const content = (
   214	    <>
   215	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   216	      <div className={`phone-screen${screenImageSrc ? " has-image" : ""}`}>
   217	        {screenImageSrc ? (
   218	          <div className="phone-screen-image-frame">
   219	            <Image
   220	              alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   221	              className="phone-screen-image"
   222	              fill
   223	              sizes="245px"
   224	              src={screenImageSrc}
   225	            />
   226	          </div>
   227	        ) : (
   228	          <>
   229	            <div className="phone-status">
   230	              <span>{title}</span>
   231	              <span>5G</span>
   232	            </div>
   233	            <div className="phone-chart">
   234	              <div />
   235	              <div />
   236	              <div />
   237	              <div />
   238	            </div>
   239	            <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   240	            <div className="phone-cardline" />
   241	            <div className="phone-grid">
   242	              <span />
   243	              <span />
   244	              <span />
   245	              <span />
   246	            </div>
   247	            <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
   248	          </>
   249	        )}
   250	      </div>
   251	    </>
   252	  );
   253	
   254	  if (href) {
   255	    return (
   256	      <a
   257	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
   258	        className={className}
   259	        href={href}
   260	      >
   261	        {content}
   262	      </a>
   263	    );
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
   267	}

    75	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    76	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    77	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    78	  ).matches;
    79	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    80	
    81	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    82	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  if (isPortrait) {
    86	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    90	}
    91	
    92	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    93	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    94	
    95	  if (!targetCard) {
    96	    return;
    97	  }
    98	
    99	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
   100	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   101	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   102	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   103	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   104	          return false;
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   108	
   109	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   110	      })
   111	    : [targetCard];
   112	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   113	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   114	  );
   115	
   116	  window.scrollTo({
   117	    behavior: "smooth",
   118	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   119	  });
   120	
   121	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   122	}

   390	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   391	  assetGap,
   392	  color,
   393	  hasTablet,
   394	  hasWeb,
   395	  mobilePlatforms,
   396	  responsiveAssetGap,
   397	  showPlatformLabels,
   398	  webIconScale,
   399	}: {
   400	  assetGap?: string;
   401	  color: string;
   402	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   403	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   404	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   405	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   406	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   407	  webIconScale?: number;
   408	}) {
   409	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   410	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   411	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   412	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   413	  } = {
   414	    color,
   415	  };
   416	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   417	
   418	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   419	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   420	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   421	      return;
   422	    }
   423	
   424	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   425	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   426	
   427	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   428	      return;
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    let frame = 0;
   432	    const updateGap = () => {
   433	      if (frame) {
   434	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   435	      }
   436	
   437	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   438	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   439	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   440	          1,
   441	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   442	        );
   443	        const progress = Math.min(
   444	          1,
   445	          Math.max(
   446	            0,
   447	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   448	          ),
   449	        );
   450	        const nextGap =
   451	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   452	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   453	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   454	
   455	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   456	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   457	        );
   458	      });
   459	    };
   460	
   461	    updateGap();
   462	
   463	    const resizeObserver =
   464	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   465	        ? null
   466	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   467	
   468	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   469	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   470	
   471	    return () => {
   472	      if (frame) {
   473	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   474	      }
   475	
   476	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   477	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   478	    };
   479	  }, [
   480	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   481	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   482	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   483	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   484	  ]);
   485	
   486	  if (stackGap) {
   487	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   488	  }
   489	
   490	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   491	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   492	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   493	
   494	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   495	      platforms.push("android");
   496	    }
   497	
   498	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   499	      platforms.push("apple");
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    if (hasWeb) {
   503	      platforms.push("web");
   504	    }
   505	
   506	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   507	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   508	    }
   509	
   510	    if (hasTablet) {
   511	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   512	    }
   513	
   514	    return (
   515	      <span
   516	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   517	        ref={stackRef}
   518	        style={stackStyle}
   519	      >
   520	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-column">
   521	          <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading">PLAT:</span>
   522	          <span className="case-card-title-platform-items">
   523	            {platforms.map((platform) =>
   524	              platform === "web" ? (
   525	                <PlatformIconSlot key={platform} label="WEB">
   526	                  <WebMark scale={webIconScale} />
   527	                </PlatformIconSlot>
   528	              ) : (
   529	                <PlatformIconSlot
   530	                  key={platform}
   531	                  label={platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS"}
   532	                >
   533	                  <PlatformLogoMark platform={platform} />
   534	                </PlatformIconSlot>
   535	              ),
   536	            )}
   537	          </span>
   538	        </span>
   539	        {factorDevices.length > 0 ? (
   540	          <span className="case-card-title-platform-column">
   541	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading">FACTOR</span>
   542	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-items">
   543	              {factorDevices.map((device) => (
   544	                <PlatformIconSlot
   545	                  key={device}
   546	                  label={device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET"}
   547	                >
   548	                  <FactorDeviceMark device={device} />
   549	                </PlatformIconSlot>
   550	              ))}
   551	            </span>
   552	          </span>
   553	        ) : null}
   554	      </span>
   555	    );
   556	  }
   557	
   558	  return (
   559	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   560	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   561	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   562	      ))}
   563	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   564	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   565	    </span>
   566	  );
   567	}
   568	
   569	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   570	  return (
   571	    <img
   572	      alt=""
   573	      aria-hidden
   574	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   575	      draggable={false}
   576	      style={{
   577	        display: "block",
   578	        height: "100%",
   579	        verticalAlign: "top",
   580	        width: "100%",
   581	      }}
   582	      src="/images/carousel-arrow.svg"
   583	    />
   584	  );
   585	}
   586	
   587	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   588	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   589	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   590	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   591	
   592	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   593	    event.preventDefault();
   594	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   595	  };
   596	
   597	  useEffect(() => {
   598	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   599	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   600	    }, 5000);
   601	
   602	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   603	  }, []);
   604	
   605	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   606	    let frame = 0;
   607	
   608	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   609	      if (frame) {
   610	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   611	      }
   612	
   613	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   614	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   615	        const heroActions =
   616	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   617	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   618	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   619	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   620	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   621	        const profilePhoto =
   622	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   623	        const socialButton =
   624	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   625	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   626	          ".profile-photo",
   627	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   628	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   629	          ".tablet-shell",
   630	        ];
   631	
   632	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   633	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   634	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   635	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   636	
   637	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
   638	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
   639	              element?.style.setProperty(
   640	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
   641	                `${proofWidth}px`,
   642	              );
   643	            });
   644	          }
   645	        }
   646	
   647	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
   648	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
   649	            "(min-width: 981px)",
   650	          ).matches;
   651	
   652	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
   653	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
   654	          } else {
   655	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
   656	              .map((selector) =>
   657	                document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
   658	              )
   659	              .filter((element): element is HTMLElement => Boolean(element))
   660	              .map((element) => element.getBoundingClientRect());
   661	
   662	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
   663	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
   664	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
   665	              const visualTop = Math.min(
   666	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
   667	              );
   668	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
   669	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
   670	              );
   671	              const currentOffset =
   672	                Number.parseFloat(
   673	                  window
   674	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
   675	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
   676	                ) || 0;
   677	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
   678	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
   679	              const targetCenter = (navRect.top + stackRect.bottom) / 2;
   680	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
   681	
   682	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
   683	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
   684	                `${nextOffset}px`,
   685	              );
   686	            }
   687	          }
   688	        }
   689	
   690	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
   691	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   692	          const socialButtonHeight =
   693	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
   694	
   695	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
   696	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
   697	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
   698	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
   699	            );
   700	          }
   701	
   702	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
   703	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
   704	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
   705	            };
   706	
   707	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
   708	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
   709	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
   710	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
   711	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
   712	            const stretchedSocialGap =
   713	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
   714	              0.091666;
   715	
   716	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
   717	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
   718	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
   719	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   720	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
   721	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
   722	            );
   723	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   724	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
   725	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
   726	            );
   727	            setPx(
   728	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
   729	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
   730	            );
   731	            setPx(
   732	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
   733	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
   734	            );
   735	            setPx(
   736	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
   737	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
   738	            );
   739	            setPx(
   740	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
   741	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
   742	            );
   743	          }
   744	        }
   745	
   746	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
   747	
   748	        const viewLabel =
   749	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   750	        const githubText =
   751	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   752	        const githubIcon =
   753	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   754	
   755	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
   756	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
   757	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
   758	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
   759	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
   760	          const clusterRight = Math.max(
   761	            githubTextRect.right,
   762	            githubIconRect.right,
   763	          );
   764	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
   765	          const stackWidth = clusterRight - clusterLeft;
   766	
   767	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
   768	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
   769	            `${stackLeft}px`,
   770	          );
   771	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
   772	            "--hero-view-stack-width",
   773	            `${stackWidth}px`,
   774	          );
   775	        }
   776	      });
   777	    };
   778	
   779	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
   780	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   781	
   782	    const targetIcon =
   783	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
   784	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
   785	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   786	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   787	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   788	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   789	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   790	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   791	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
   792	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   793	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   794	    const githubIcon =
   795	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   796	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
   797	    const resizeObserver =
   798	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   799	        ? null
   800	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
   801	
   802	    if (resizeObserver) {
   803	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
   804	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
   805	      }
   806	
   807	      if (heroActions) {
   808	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
   809	      }
   810	
   811	      if (navBar) {
   812	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
   813	      }
   814	
   815	      if (heroVisual) {
   816	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
   817	      }
   818	
   819	      if (stackRow) {
   820	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
   821	      }
   822	
   823	      if (proofPanel) {
   824	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
   825	      }
   826	
   827	      if (profilePhoto) {
   828	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
   829	      }
   830	
   831	      if (targetIcon) {
   832	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
   833	      }
   834	
   835	      if (targetButton) {
   836	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
   837	      }
   838	
   839	      if (viewLabel) {
   840	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
   841	      }
   842	
   843	      if (githubText) {
   844	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
   845	      }
   846	
   847	      if (githubIcon) {
   848	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
   849	      }
   850	
   851	      if (githubButton) {
   852	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
   853	      }
   854	    }
   855	
   856	    if ("fonts" in document) {
   857	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
   858	    }
   859	
   860	    return () => {
   861	      if (frame) {
   862	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   863	      }
   864	
   865	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   866	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   867	    };
   868	  }, []);
   869	
   870	  return (
   871	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
   872	      <a
   873	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
   874	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
   875	        href={activeProject.href}
   876	        ref={projectButtonRef}
   877	      >
   878	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
   879	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
   880	            <span
   881	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
   882	              style={{
   883	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
   884	                alignItems: "center",
   885	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
   886	                display: "inline-flex",
   887	                flexDirection: "column",
   888	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
   889	                gap: "2.2px",
   890	                justifyContent: "center",
   891	                lineHeight: 0.95,
   892	              }}
   893	            >
   894	              <span
   895	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
   896	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
   897	              >
   898	                {activeProject.name}
   899	              </span>
   900	              <span
   901	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
   902	                style={{
   903	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
   904	                  color: "#000",
   905	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
   906	                  fontWeight: 900,
   907	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
   908	                  lineHeight: 1,
   909	                  textAlign: "center",
   910	                  textShadow: "none",
   911	                }}
   912	              >
   913	                {activeProject.category}
   914	              </span>
   915	            </span>
   916	            <ProjectDeviceStack
   917	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
   918	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
   919	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
   920	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
   921	            />
   922	          </span>
   923	        </span>
   924	      </a>
   925	      <a
   926	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
   927	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
   928	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
   929	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
   930	      >
   931	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
   932	          <CarouselArrowMark />
   933	        </span>
   934	      </a>
   935	    </span>
   936	  );
   937	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- out
    54	- dist
    55	- build
    56	- .cache
    57	- .turbo
    58	- .npm
    59	- .pnpm-store
    60	- .yarn
    61	- .DS_Store
    62	- next-env.d.ts
    63	- *.tsbuildinfo
    64	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    65	- *.zip
    66	- .env and .env*.local
    67	
    68	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    69	
    70	
    71	============================================================
    72	PROJECT FILE TREE
    73	============================================================
    74	
    75	EOF
    76	}
    77	
    78	print_file() {
    79	  local file="$1"
    80	
    81	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    82	    {
    83	      echo ""
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo "============================================================"
    86	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    87	      echo "============================================================"
    88	      echo ""
    89	      nl -ba "$file"
    90	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    91	  fi
    92	}
    93	
    94	print_inventory_header() {
    95	  local title="$1"
    96	
    97	  {
    98	    echo ""
    99	    echo ""
   100	    echo "============================================================"
   101	    echo "$title"
   102	    echo "============================================================"
   103	    echo ""
   104	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   105	}
   106	
   107	print_file_inventory_line() {
   108	  local file="$1"
   109	  local size
   110	  local modified
   111	
   112	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   113	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   114	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   115	}
   116	
   117	print_dir_inventory() {
   118	  local title="$1"
   119	  local dir="$2"
   120	
   121	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   122	
   123	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   124	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   125	    return
   126	  fi
   127	
   128	  find "$dir" -type f \
   129	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   130	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   131	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   132	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   133	  done
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_matching_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  shift
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   143	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   144	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   145	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   146	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   147	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   148	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   149	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   150	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   151	  done
   152	}
   153	
   154	append_git_context() {
   155	  {
   156	    echo ""
   157	    echo ""
   158	    echo "============================================================"
   159	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   160	    echo "============================================================"
   161	    echo ""
   162	    echo "Current branch:"
   163	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   164	    echo ""
   165	    echo "Status:"
   166	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   167	    echo ""
   168	    echo "Recent commits:"
   169	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   170	    echo ""
   171	    echo "Remotes:"
   172	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   173	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   174	}
   175	
   176	write_snapshot() {
   177	  write_header
   178	
   179	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   180	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   181	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules" -prune -o \
   182	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next" -prune -o \
   183	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out" -prune -o \
   184	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/dist" -prune -o \

- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
